## 新投稿 (166)

<h3 id="1">[1] In Search of the Ingredients of Open-Endedness: Replicating Picbreeder with Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23908)
- **Authors**: Sam Earle, Kay Arulkumaran, Andrew Dai, Akarsh Kumar, Julian Togelius, Sebastian Risi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Open-Ended Evolution, Evolutionary Computation
- **Venue**: GECCO 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/smearle/picbreeder-vlm)
- **Summary**: 本文用视觉语言模型替代人类用户复制Picbreeder实验，研究开放式发现的特征。研究发现AI与人类输出存在明显定性差异，并探讨了探索性噪声、智能体行为多样性和叙事动量等因素的影响。

<h3 id="2">[2] Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23909)
- **Authors**: Noam Michael, Daniel BenShushan, Jacob Bien, Don A. Moore
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Calibration, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文研究大语言模型在不同任务中的置信度校准问题，发现模型像人类一样过度自信，且过度自信在困难测试中最明显，而在简单测试中反而出现不自信现象。作者开发了LifeEval测试框架来评估模型在不同难度下的校准表现。

<h3 id="3">[3] How Much Thinking is Enough? Quantifying and Understanding Redundancy in LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23926](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23926)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Zhai, Xinkai You, Wenjing Yan, Xin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/how-much-thinking-is-enough)
- **Summary**: 本文量化并解释了大语言模型推理中的冗余问题，发现61%-93%的推理步骤是冗余的。作者证明了这种冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性后果，而非模型特定的伪影，因此过度思考不是可以修补的bug而是当前推理模型训练方式的结构特性。

<h3 id="4">[4] Context: Proactive Goal-Directed Intelligence via Composable Sandboxed Programs, Declarative Wiring, and Structured Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23928)
- **Authors**: Gregory Magarshak
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DC; cs.MA; cs.PL; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Proactive Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出Context架构，用主动目标导向的智能体替代被动响应式聊天机器人。该架构基于三个机制：写入时上下文组装、可组合沙盒程序和主动目标流状态机，并证明了六个形式化结果。

<h3 id="5">[5] Toward Reliable Design of LLM-Enabled Agentic Workflows: Optimizing Latency-Reliability-Cost Tradeoffs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23929)
- **Authors**: Ya-Ting Yang, Quanyan Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文分析了LLM驱动的智能体工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间的基本权衡，引入了LLM和非LLM智能体的性能模型，并提出了水填充令牌分配策略。

<h3 id="6">[6] Quantum Frog: Emergent Cooperation and Difficulty Scaling in a Quantized-Time Cooperative Game</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23930)
- **Authors**: Saad Mankarious
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Game AI, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Quantum Frog，一个基于量子化时间机制的两人合作游戏。通过强化学习分析游戏难度扩展、最优策略和合作差距，发现同步冲刺是涌现的合作策略。

<h3 id="7">[7] BODHI: Precise OS Kernel Specification Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23931)
- **Authors**: Zhiming Chang, Ziyang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.PL; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Code Generation, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出BODHI方法，通过领域知识提示来推断操作系统内核规范。在OSV-Bench基准测试中，最佳配置达到96.73%的Pass@1，证明了领域知识注入是模型无关的有效技术。

<h3 id="8">[8] When Correct Beliefs Collapse: Epistemic Resilience of LLMs under Clinical Pressure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23932)
- **Authors**: Boyu Xiao, Xiuqi Tian, Xuwen Song, Haochun Wang, Guanchun Song, Sendong Zhao, Bing Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, AI Safety
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出Med-Stress压力测试框架，评估LLM在临床对话中的信念稳定性。研究发现医学知识与鲁棒性之间存在分离，并提出了RBED和R-FT两种防御方法来缓解信念崩溃问题。

<h3 id="9">[9] Practical Quantum CIM Empowerment via All-Domestic-Core Agentic Large Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23934)
- **Authors**: Wang Rui, Lu Diannan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文将飞秒激光泵浦相干伊辛机与LLM驱动的智能体系统集成，实现了QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重迭代和文献方案快速验证等任务，完全基于国产大模型和硬件实现了量子CIM的实际赋能。

<h3 id="10">[10] Operationalizing Reconstructive Authority: Runtime Construction, Dependency Resolution, and Execution Gating in Autonomous Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23935)
- **Authors**: Marcelo Fernandez - TraslaIA
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA; cs.SE; eess.SY
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文引入运行时执行模型，在动作执行时评估权限，扩展了执行状态空间以包含暂停状态。定义了包含动态依赖解析、权限重构和显式决策语义的具体执行协议，并引入恢复循环来处理权限未定义的情况。

<h3 id="11">[11] Fuzzy, Neutrosophic, and Uncertain Graph Theory: Properties and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23936)
- **Authors**: Takaaki Fujita, Florentin Smarandache
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Knowledge Graph, Survey
- **Summary**: 本书全面系统地综述了不确定性下的图论，重点关注不确定图框架的统一作用。涵盖了模糊、中智及相关模型的基本概念、结构性质、图类和图参数，并探讨了在分子图、决策系统、图神经网络、知识图谱和认知地图中的应用。

<h3 id="15">[15] Residual Drift Dominates Contradiction in Multi-Turn Constraint Reasoning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Multi-Turn Interaction, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/kaons-research/drift-bench)
- **Summary**: 本文发现多轮推理系统的主要失败模式是"可满足漂移"而非逻辑矛盾，即系统内部状态一致但返回答案违反先前承诺。作者构建了DRIFT-Bench基准测试，并提出MUS-Repair方法，发现修复后98-100%的残余错误属于可满足漂移。

- **Authors**: Samah Fodeh, Sreeraj Ramachandran, Elyas Irankhah, Muhammad Arif, Afshan Khan, Ganesh Puthiaraju, Linhai Ma, Srivani Talakokkul, Jordan Alpert, Sarah Schellhorn

<h3 id="44">[44] A Sober Look at Agentic Misalignment in Automated Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24197)

<h3 id="45">[45] When Does Multi-Agent RL Improve LLM Workflows? Workflow, Scale, and Policy-Sharing Tradeoffs</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文研究多智能体RL何时能改进LLM工作流，比较共享策略训练和隔离策略训练。研究发现多智能体RL通常能改进基础模型，但收益取决于工作流、任务和规模的联合作用，隔离策略倾向于达到更高峰值准确率但更易出现准确率断崖。

<h3 id="46">[46] Identifying and Mitigating Systemic Measurement Bias in Production LLM Inference Benchmarks</h3>

<h3 id="47">[47] Beyond Final Answers: Auditing Trajectory-Level Hallucinations in Multi-Agent Industrial Workflows</h3>

<h3 id="48">[48] How Well Do Models Follow Their Constitutions?</h3>

<h3 id="49">[49] Toward Enactive Artificial Intelligence</h3>

<h3 id="50">[50] Safety-Oriented Routing Analysis of Mixtral MoE Under Benign and Harmful Prompts</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR

<h3 id="51">[51] When Does Synthetic Patent Data Help? Volume-Fidelity Trade-offs in Low-Resource Multi-Label Classification</h3>

<h3 id="81">[81] GlobalDentBench: A Multinational Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Clinical Reasoning in Dentistry with Expert Calibration</h3>

- **Authors**: Junjie Zhao, Jingyi Liang, Zhenyang Cai, Jiaming Zhang, Zhenwei Wen, Shuzhi Deng, Wenjing Yi, Chunfeng Luo, Hexian Zhang, Junying Chen, Tianrui Liu, Zhuhui Bai, Zixu Zhang, Pradeep Singh, Xiang Liu, Jianquan Li, Nhan L Tran, Falk Schwendicke, Zuolin Jin, Lijian Jin, Liangyi Chen, Wei-fa Yang, Benyou Wang, Junwen Wang, Shan Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了GlobalDentBench，这是首个跨国牙科基准测试，涵盖14个牙科专业、88个国家和地区的8,978个专家验证问题。对12个前沿LLM的评估显示，随着推理复杂度增加性能急剧下降，且在临床推荐中存在31.01%的不安全率。

<h3 id="82">[82] AVBench: Human-Aligned and Automated Evaluation Benchmark for Audio-Video Generative Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.MM; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Benchmark, Audio Processing, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了AVBench，一个针对以人为中心的音视频生成模型的自动化评估基准，包含十个细粒度评估维度和通过偏好学习训练的专业评估器。该基准提供与人类判断高度一致的连续评估分数，并可作为RLHF的可微分奖励信号。

<h3 id="83">[83] Beyond Inference-Only Deployment: Comparing Weight-Based Consolidation Against Cascading Compaction</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Fine-Tuning, Memory Architecture, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文比较了权重整合与级联压缩两种LLM个性化方法，发现权重整合保留了80.4%的知识，而级联压缩仅保留36.8%。研究还发现中位数交叉熵比均值交叉熵更能准确追踪模型质量。

<h3 id="84">[84] Measuring Reasoning Quality in LLMs: A Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Framework</h3>

<h3 id="85">[85] When Mean CE Fails: Median CE Can Better Track Language Model Quality</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文证明在合成事实学习SFT和Top-K蒸馏等场景中，中位数交叉熵比均值交叉熵更能准确追踪语言模型质量。作者建议在训练过程中报告百分位交叉熵摘要，以监测分布重塑情况。

<h3 id="86">[86] Exploration of Perceptual Speech Features for Clinical Decision-Support in Mental Health Care</h3>

<h3 id="87">[87] Emotional intelligence in large language models is fragmented across perception, cognition, and interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24686)

<h3 id="88">[88] MDIA: A Multi-Agent Diagnostic Intelligence Pipeline on HealthBench Professional</h3>

<h3 id="89">[89] Fundamental Limitation in Explaining AI</h3>

- **Tags**: Explainable AI, AI Safety, Interpretability, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本文从数学上证明了AI解释的根本四难困境：AI系统及其解释无法同时满足环境复杂性、良好性能、可解释性和完全忠实性四个条件。这一发现表明AI治理应基于解释忠实性始终不完整的前提进行设计。

<h3 id="90">[90] Hylos: Operability Contracts for Model-Native Spatial Intelligence</h3>

<h3 id="91">[91] Automated Detection and Classification of Delusion-related Content in Naturalistic Audio Diaries Using Multi-Agent Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="97">[97] CoRe-Code: Collaborative Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation</h3>

<h3 id="98">[98] Agent Manufacturing: Foundation-Model Agents as First-Class Industrial Entities</h3>

<h3 id="99">[99] Test-Time Deep Thinking to Explore Implicit Rules</h3>

<h3 id="100">[100] Geo-Expert: Towards Expert-Level Geological Reasoning via Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning</h3>

<h3 id="101">[101] Solving Combinatorial Counting Problems with Weighted First-Order Model Counting</h3>

<h3 id="102">[102] Clustering as Reasoning: A $k$-Means Interpretation of Chain-of-Thought Graph Learning</h3>

<h3 id="104">[104] TaBIIC2: Interactive Building of Ontological Taxonomies using Weighted Self-Organizing Maps</h3>

<h3 id="105">[105] ProActor: Timing-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Proactive Task Scheduling Agents</h3>

<h3 id="107">[107] Energy Shields for Fairness</h3>

<h3 id="108">[108] Towards Multi-Turn Dialog Systems for Industrial Asset Operations and Maintenance</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理策略，通过区域感知注意力重校准来缓解视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉问题，在多个基准测试上取得了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="110">[110] NeurIPS: Neuro-anatomical Inductive Priors for Sphere-based Brain Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24993](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24993)
- **Authors**: Sijin Yu, Zijiao Chen, Zhenyu Yang, Zihao Tan, Jiakun Xu, Zhongliang Liu, Shengxian Chen, Wenxuan Wu, Xiangmin Xu, Xin Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, Mixture-of-Experts, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出NeurIPS框架，通过选择性ROI球形标记器和结构引导的专家混合模型，将解剖变异作为归纳偏置用于球面fMRI解码。该方法在表面解码器中达到最优性能，收敛速度提升60倍（10 vs 600轮）。

<h3 id="111">[111] Privacy-Preserving Local Language Models for Longitudinal Data Retrieval in Chronic Dermatologic Disease: Implementation in Pemphigus Patients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25020)
- **Authors**: Abdurrahim Yilmaz, Ayşe Esra Koku Aksu, Duygu Yamen, Vefa Asli Erdemir, Mehmet Salih Gurel, Gulsum Gencoglan, Joram M. Posma, Burak Temelkuran
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Privacy, Small Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文评估了本地部署的小语言模型（Qwen3 4B）用于从慢性皮肤病随访记录中检索临床特征和生成纵向摘要。在1680个特征检索任务中平均准确率达82.25%，皮肤科医生对AI摘要的质量、准确性和实用性评分较高。

<h3 id="112">[112] AION: Next-Generation Tasks and Practical Harness for Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25045](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25045)
- **Authors**: Tianxiang Zhan, Xiaobao Song, Tong Guan, Shirui Pan, Ming Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ztxtech/aion)
- **Summary**: 本文将下一代时间序列任务形式化为三元组（任务文件、工作空间、验证接口），并提出AION框架，包含智能体、技能、规则、记忆、评估和协议六个组件组。案例研究表明该框架产生更详细的流程追踪和更多工件。

<h3 id="113">[113] Evolutionary Enhanced Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Air Combat</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25091)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, Autonomous Systems
- **Summary**: 本文提出ACE-MAPPO框架，将进化算法与MAPPO结合用于无人机协同空战决策，通过遗传软更新机制、进化增强优先轨迹回放和对抗进化课程学习来提升探索效率、样本利用率和策略泛化能力。

<h3 id="114">[114] RECTOR: Priority-Aware Rule-Based Reranking for Compliance-Aware Autonomous Driving Trajectory Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25095)
- **Authors**: Hadi Hajieghrary, Benedikt Walter, Chaitanya Shinde, Paul Schmitt, Miguel Hurtado
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; math.OC
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Trajectory Prediction, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出RECTOR，一种用于自动驾驶轨迹选择的后生成重排序层，通过分层规则手册（安全\>法律\>道路\>舒适）和可微分代理对候选轨迹进行评分，使用确定性ε-字典序规则选择，显著减少安全和法律违规。

<h3 id="115">[115] Trust but Verify: Prover-Verifier Deliberation for Selective LLM Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25133)
- **Authors**: João Sedoc, Baotong Zhang, Dean Foster
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出证明者-验证者审议(PVD)协议，一种用于选择性预测的推理时方法，证明者通过可检查的子声明辩护候选答案，验证者发出针对性挑战，产生答案和结构化置信度裁决，实现高置信度答案报告和不确定情况弃权。

<h3 id="116">[116] Beyond the Frontier: Stochastic Backtracking for Efficient Test-Time Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25143)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Test-Time Scaling, LLM Reasoning, Inference Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出在历史前缀持久池上进行随机回溯的测试时扩展方法，允许推理计算重新访问之前生成的状态，引入子池选择和幂回溯序贯蒙特卡洛两种机制，在数学推理基准上以更少token数达到更高准确率。

<h3 id="117">[117] Representation Without Control: Testing the Realization Effect in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25151)
- **Authors**: Ciarán Walsh, Emilio Barkett
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 本文通过行为经济学中的实现效应研究LLM是否反映类人认知机制，发现Gemma的残差流包含可线性解码的实现状态信号，但沿此方向进行激活引导不能可靠地改变下游风险选择，表明行为敏感性、潜在读取和因果控制是三种不同属性。

<h3 id="118">[118] SimuWoB: Simulating Real-World Mobile Apps for Fast and Faithful GUI Agent Benchmarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25160)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文介绍SimuWoB，一个用于移动GUI代理的完全合成基准，包含120个跨越多种类型和难度级别的挑战性任务，构建了高保真任务和环境合成框架并自动提供有效奖励，揭示当前代理在复杂场景下成功率仅27.92%。

<h3 id="119">[119] SpecAlign: A Semantic Alignment Framework for SystemVerilog Assertion Generation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="120">[120] DarkForest: Less Talk, Higher Accuracy for Multi-Agent LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25188)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出DarkForest，一种受控通信的多代理LLM协调框架，保持代理独立性后将原始响应解析为结构化候选记录并估计校准置信度分布，相比通信密集型基线提高30.7%基准指标并减少6.5倍token消耗。

<h3 id="121">[121] Boosting Inference with Guided Reasoning: Stochastic Exploration for Recursive Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25230)
- **Authors**: Andrew Corbett, Archit Sood, Anna Tzatzopoulou, Sai-Aakash Ramesh, Tim Dodwell
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Inference Optimization, Neural Combinatorial Optimization, Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出引导随机探索方法用于递归神经网络，将确定性递归视为潜在推理轨迹近似推断的单粒子零噪声极限，框架产生三个无标签诊断指标，在Sudoku-Extreme上将准确率从85.9%提升至98.0%而无需重新训练。

<h3 id="122">[122] Meta-Agent: From Task Descriptions to Verified Multi-Agent Systems</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出Meta-Agent，一个从自然语言任务描述自动构建和执行专用多代理系统的两阶段框架，包含构建时和执行时验证阶段以及三级错误归因机制，在编码、上下文学习和开放推理任务上展示了一致的任务成功率和错误恢复改进。

<h3 id="123">[123] FrontierOR: Benchmarking LLMs' Capacity for Efficient Algorithm Design in Large-Scale Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25246)
- **Authors**: Minwei Kong, Chonghe Jiang, Ao Qu, Wenbin Ouyang, Zhaoming Zeng, Xiaotong Guo, Zhekai Li, Junyi Li, Yi Fan, Xinshou Zheng, Xi Jing, Yikai Zhang, Zhiwei Liang, Seonghoo Kim, Runqing Yang, Zijian Zhou, Sirui Li, Han Zheng, Wangyang Ying, Ou Zheng, Chonghuan Wang, Jinglong Zhao, Hanzhang Qin, Cathy Wu, Paul Pu Liang, Jinhua Zhao, Hai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Optimization, LLM Reasoning

<h3 id="124">[124] LipoAgent: Coordinating Fine-Tuned LLM Agents for Safer Lipid Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25250)
- **Authors**: Leshu Li, An Lu, Haiyu Wang, Zhibin Feng, Conghui Duan, Qing Bao, Zongmin Zhao, Sai Qian Zhang

<h3 id="125">[125] Whose Alignment? Comparing LLM Process Alignment Across Diverse Organizational Decision Contexts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25256)
- **Authors**: Niklas Weller, Emilio Barkett
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Evaluation, Fairness
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文通过过程对齐测量方法研究LLM是否像组织一样加权信息，应用于ECHR第6条决定时过程对齐强预测输出准确率，但应用于德国消费信贷决定时该关系消失，表明输出协议本身无法区分内化政策与近似结果。

<h3 id="126">[126] AI Cartography: Mapping the Latent Landscape of AI Benchmark Ecosystems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25272)
- **Authors**: Michael Hardy, Anka Reuel, Lijin Zhang, Jodi M. Casabianca, Sang Truong, Yash Dave, Hansol Lee, Benjamin Domingue, Sanmi Koyejo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文应用验证性因子分析和概化理论于Open LLM排行榜的4000多个模型，分解排名方差来源，发现排行榜项目存在局部依赖性、贡献者元数据解释的排名相关方差比架构类别更多、显式分数缩放定律斜率可靠性低而潜在一般因子斜率高度稳定。

<h3 id="127">[127] Context-CoT: Enhancing Context Learning via High-Quality Reasoning Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25354](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25354)
- **Authors**: Hongbo Jin, Mingnan Zhu, Jingqi Tian, Xu Jiang, Zhongjing Du, Haoran Tang, Siyi Xie, Qiaoman Zhang, Jiayu Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="128">[128] Second Guess: Detecting Uncertainty Through Abstention and Answer Stability in Small Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25394](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25394)
- **Authors**: Ashwath Vaithinathan Aravindan, Mayank Kejriwal

- **Summary**: 本文提出Second Guess，一种适用于小语言模型的多选题问答弃权轻量级无参数提示技术，核心洞察是真正知道答案的模型会一致选择该答案，而不确定模型在添加我不知道选项时表现出不稳定行为，实现10.81%的综合风险改进。

<h3 id="129">[129] Towards end-to-end LLM-based censoring-aware survival analysis</h3>

<h3 id="130">[130] CODESKILL: Learning Self-Evolving Skills for Coding Agents</h3>

<h3 id="131">[131] Security of OpenClaw Agents: Fundamentals, Attacks, and Countermeasures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25435)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文对OpenClaw智能体（一类具有持久记忆和高自主性的开源智能体框架）的安全问题进行全面调研，将安全威胁分类为分层框架，分析了智能体推理、动作执行和外部交互过程中的漏洞及防御机制。

<h3 id="132">[132] A Signal-Language Foundation Model for Broad-Spectrum Cardiovascular Assessment from Routine Electrocardiography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25446)
- **Authors**: Ziqing Yu, Yuhui Tao, Jiayu Huo, Lei Pan, Zilong Xiao, Juecheng Chen, Xiao Li, Jianxuan Li, You Zhou, Zhixing Li, Cong Wang, Beijian Zhang, Chen Chen, Hongyang Lu, Konstantinos Patlatzoglou, Daniel B. Kramer, Jonathan W. Waks, Yangang Su, Fu Siong Ng, Shuo Wang, Yixiu Liang, Junbo Ge
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Foundation Model, ECG Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出ECGCLIP，一种信号-语言对比学习框架，通过将心电图波形与专家诊断报告对齐，在280万份心电图研究上预训练。模型在89个下游任务中表现优异，涵盖心律失常检测、超声心动图目标和罕见心脏病诊断。

<h3 id="133">[133] ATWL: A Formal Language for Representing, Comparing, and Reusing Visual Analytics Workflows</h3>

<h3 id="134">[134] Credit Assignment with Resets in Language Model Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25507](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25507)
- **Authors**: Ankur Samanta, Akshayaa Magesh, Ayush Jain, Youliang Yu, Daniel Jiang, Kavosh Asadi, Daniel Jiang, Kaveh Hassani, Paul Sajda, Jalaj Bhandari, Yonathan Efroni
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="135">[135] What Gets Cited: Competitive GEO in AI Answer Engines</h3>

- **Authors**: Rahul Vishwakarma, Shushant Kumar, Ratnesh Jamidar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="136">[136] StructBreak: Structural Cognitive Overload-Induced Safety Failures in MLLMs</h3>

<h3 id="137">[137] Personalize-then-Store: Benchmarking and Learning Personalized Memory for Long-horizon Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="138">[138] ADMFormer: An Adaptive-Decomposition Transformer with Time-Varying Masked Spatial Attention for Traffic Forecasting</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出ADMFormer，一种自适应分解Transformer，通过时间-节点自适应门控解耦交通信号为规律性和波动性成分，并采用时变掩码空间注意力来稀疏化空间交互，在四个数据集上达到最优性能。

<h3 id="139">[139] PHGNet: Prototype-Guided Hypergraph Construction for Heterogeneous Spatiotemporal Forecasting</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Forecasting, Graph Neural Network, Spatiotemporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出PHGNet，一种基于原型引导超图构建的时空预测框架，通过原型学习机制自适应地将模式相似节点分配到超边以捕获高阶交互，结合迭代残差细化和时序查询注意力提升预测精度。

<h3 id="140">[140] Beyond Query Memorization: Large Language Model Routing with Query Decomposition and Historical Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25558](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25558)
- **Authors**: Bo Lv, Jingbo Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Routing, LLM Inference, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lvbotenbest/DecoR)

- **Summary**: 本文提出VineLM工作流管理器，通过基于Trie的模型选择前缀表示和级联分析，在智能体工作流执行过程中为每个阶段调用动态选择模型，在成本或延迟预算下优化准确率。

<h3 id="174">[174] Agent-Facing Information Design in LLM Tool Registries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23916) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haochuan Kevin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; econ.GN
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, LLM Agent, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM工具注册表作为无监管广告平台的问题，发现法律夸大宣传捕获100%的优化效果，提出将选择面向描述与营销面向描述分离，并引入智能体注意力质量分数。

<h3 id="175">[175] Artificial Effort</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23920) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Federico Belotti, Stefano Coniglio, Antonio Cosma, Francesco Fallucchi
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Human-Computer Interaction, Experimental Economics
- **Summary**: 本文研究了AI时代实验经济学中真实努力任务的有效性，使用8个经典任务和23个LLM发现大多数任务可被低成本自动化，为无监督环境下使用此类任务确立了边界条件。

<h3 id="176">[176] Authority Signals in Claude AI Health Citations: A Descriptive Analysis Using the Authority Signals Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23921) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, Fact Checking
- **Summary**: 本文使用权威信号框架分析Claude AI健康引用中的权威信号，发现97.8%的引用来自机构来源，医疗机构是最常被引用的组织类型，为Claude的引用行为建立了基线。

<h3 id="177">[177] High-Risk AI Systems and the Problem of Identity in the European AI Act</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23922) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrea Ferrario
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Policy
- **Venue**: FAccT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文分析了欧盟AI法案中对高风险AI系统的生命周期治理机制，重点关注AI系统身份判断问题。作者提出了function+框架，为监管环境下的AI系统身份判定提供了可审计的标准。

<h3 id="178">[178] Catching The Correct Answer Trap: Characterising AI Tutor Blind Spots When Analysing Student Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23925) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Moiz Imran, Sahan Bulathwela
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Education Technology, LLM Evaluation, Misconception Detection
- **Venue**: AIED 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了智能辅导系统中的“正确答案陷阱”问题，即AI模型容易漏检学生通过错误推理得出正确答案时的误解。研究表明，尽管先进的大语言模型能缓解该问题，但误报率依然很高，证明高整体准确率会掩盖推理评估中的关键缺陷，仍需人类判断介入。

<h3 id="179">[179] KT4EQG: Personalized Exercise Question Generation via Knowledge Tracing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23933](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23933) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyi Gao, Qiucheng Wu, Lu Ding, Q.Vera Liao, Kaizhi Qian, Ying Xu, Shiyu Chang, Yang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Tracing, Personalized Learning, Question Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出KT4EQG框架，将知识追踪与教育问题生成相结合，通过建模学生知识状态来选择最适合的知识概念，并利用LLM生成个性化练习题目。实验结果表明，该方法能够有效生成比非个性化方法更具学习价值的问题。

<h3 id="180">[180] AI-Driven Controlled Environment Agriculture as Resilient Infrastructure for U.S. Fresh-Produce Supply Chains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23946](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23946) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrii Vakhnovskyi
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, System Design, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个评估AI驱动的受控环境农业(CEA)作为区域生鲜农产品连续性基础设施的框架(CEA-RIF 2.0)，从供应连续性、气候隔离、能源与电网整合、水资源循环等七个维度进行评估，论证了AI仅在改善气候稳定性、能源灵活性、产量一致性等可测量运营结果时才能创造韧性价值。

<h3 id="181">[181] SODE: Analyzing Social Dynamics in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23949](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23949) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Inseo Jung, Yoonseok Oh, Kyungryul Back, Jinkyu Kim, Jungbeom Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Social Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SODE框架，用于评估LLM智能体在人类社会动态中的行为对齐，从直接互惠、间接互惠和群体动态三个演化维度进行分析，发现指令微调模型表现出易被利用的被动顺从性，而推理模型优先考虑短期优化，破坏长期合作。

<h3 id="182">[182] EchoDistill:Alignment Noisy-to-Clean Self-Distillation for Robust Audio LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23954) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文提出了EchoDistill，一种基于对齐的噪声到清洁自蒸馏框架，利用冻结的清洁音频教师模型为推理时的噪声音频学生模型提供语义参考，通过组相对策略优化(GRPO)显著提升了音频大语言模型在复杂噪声条件下的语义可靠性和任务性能。

<h3 id="183">[183] AI in the Enterprise: How People Use M365 Copilot Chat</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23958](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23958) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Scott Counts, Yan Chen, Jing Dong, Himanshu Sharma, Andrey Zaikin, Rui Hu, Alperen Kok, Gorkem Ozer Yilmaz, Siddharth Suri, Kiran Tomlinson, Sonia Jaffe, Will Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; econ.GN
- **Tags**: Enterprise AI, Human-Computer Interaction, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文通过对约550万次M365 Copilot Chat会话的隐私保护分析，刻画了企业AI的使用模式，发现写作任务占主导地位，用户也依赖其进行信息检索、分析、决策和系统诊断，时间趋势显示使用正从搜索转向内容和通信相关工作。

<h3 id="184">[184] Multimodal Alignment and Preference Optimization for Zero-Shot Conditional RNA Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23961](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23961) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roman Klypa, Alberto Bietti, Sergei Grudinin
- **Subjects**: q-bio.BM; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="185">[185] Multi-market value-stacking: Battery control for combined imbalance participation and non-uniform FCR bidding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23964](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23964) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="186">[186] TriVAL: A Tri-Validation Framework for Faithful Automatic Optimization Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23966) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="187">[187] Sensing Intelligence as a Trainable Metamaterial Property</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23967) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="188">[188] Metacognition Should Be the Scientific Framework for Bounded and Effective Self-Governance in Generative AI</h3>

<h3 id="189">[189] Parameter Efficient Multi-Class Intelligent Scheduling for Multimodal Online Distributed Industrial Anomaly Detection</h3>

<h3 id="190">[190] MemForest: An Efficient Agent Memory System with Hierarchical Temporal Indexing</h3>

<h3 id="193">[193] RAW: Robust Avatar Watermarking -- Benchmarking and Baseline</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

<h3 id="194">[194] Task-Aligned Self-Supervised Learning for Medical Image Analysis: A Systematic Review and Practical Design Guidelines</h3>

<h3 id="195">[195] IVR-R1: Refining Trajectories through Iterative Visual-Grounded Reasoning in Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="196">[196] Diff-Instruct with Diffused Reward: Towards Principled One-step Generator RL</h3>

<h3 id="198">[198] Remote sensing data imputation using deep learning for multispectral imagery</h3>

<h3 id="199">[199] ActQuant: Sub-4-bit Action-Guided Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xiaotianliu-dartmouth/Iterative_Refinement_Neural_Operator)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了迭代细化神经算子IRNO，通过学习式残差修正缓解谱偏差问题。该方法在湍流等物理系统上实现了高达56%的误差降低，显著改善了高频细节的分辨率。

<h3 id="205">[205] Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander Okezue Bell
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文证明了高斯释放机制在隐藏状态隐私中无法同时实现中等效用和隐私，存在"空中间层"现象。作者提出了分裂记忆变压器架构，在隐私-效用权衡上取得了更好的平衡。

<h3 id="206">[206] LLM-AutoSciLab: Closed-Loop Scientific Discovery via Active Experimentation with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24043](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24043) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sanchit Kabra, Nikhil Abhyankar, Saaketh Desai, Prasad Iyer, Chandan K Reddy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Discovery, LLM Agent, Active Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/scientific-discovery/LLM-AutoSciLab)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了LLM-AutoSciLab闭环框架，将假设生成与主动实验选择相结合进行科学发现。该方法在酶动力学和基因调控网络任务上超越了现有方法，样本效率提高了2-5倍。

<h3 id="207">[207] A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark: Do Protein-Ligand Models Learn Binding Sites or Just Binding Likelihood?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24045](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24045) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaohan Meng, Zhen Bai, Ke Yuan, Iadh Ounis, Zaiqiao Meng, Hao Xu, Joseph Loscalzo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Benchmark, Protein Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文引入了InteractBind数据集和基准，包含约10万个蛋白质-配体对，用于细粒度结合位点定位评估。研究发现现有模型在二元结合预测上表现良好，但在结合位点定位方面能力有限。

<h3 id="208">[208] Mixture of Complementary Agents for Robust LLM Ensemble</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24048) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yichi Zhang, Kevin Lu, Yuang Zhang, Jie Gao, Lirong Xia, Fang-Yi Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Ensemble, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文将LLM集成中的提议者选择问题重构为基于互补性的组合选择问题。作者探索了多种贪心式选择算法，通过评估互补性实现了更好的性能-成本权衡。

<h3 id="209">[209] More Skills, Worse Agents? Skill Shadowing Degrades Performance When Expanding Skill Libraries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24050](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24050) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongwen Song, Song
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了技能库扩展时LLM智能体性能下降的问题，发现技能遮蔽效应（智能体错误选择技能）是主要瓶颈而非上下文开销，为技能库设计提供了重要见解。

<h3 id="210">[210] Truthful Online Preference Aggregation for LLM Fine-Tuning in Mobile Crowdsourcing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shugang Hao, Lingjie Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, Crowdsourcing, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了移动众包中LLM微调的真实在线偏好聚合问题，提出了动态调整工人权重的在线加权聚合机制，确保策略性工人提供真实反馈并实现次线性遗憾。

<h3 id="211">[211] Cascade-KDE: Robust Time-Series Restoration under Out-of-Distribution Impulse Corruptions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24055) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuefeng Liu, Ning Yang, Ziyu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Anomaly Detection, Signal Processing
- **Summary**: 该论文提出Cascade-KDE，一种无需训练的时间序列修复框架，用于处理高斯噪声和脉冲异常值的混合污染。该方法通过密度截断和级联优化机制，在保持局部特征（如导数峰值）的同时提高鲁棒性，在多个基准数据集上优于传统滤波器和学习基线。

<h3 id="212">[212] Feature Lottery? A Bifurcation Theory of Concept Emergence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24057) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fuming Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Interpretability, Grokking
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种表示动力学的分岔理论，用于实时检测神经网络训练过程中结构化表示的涌现时刻。作者引入了一个无标签相位坐标β/βc，能够早期预测特征的可解释性和训练健康状况，在语言模型、自监督学习和grokking场景中得到验证。

<h3 id="213">[213] Signs Beat Floats: Low-Rank Double-Binary Adaptation for On-Device Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24058) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yoshihiko Fujisawa, Yuma Ichikawa, Yudai Fujimoto, Akira Sakai, Katsuki Fujisawa
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 该论文提出LoRDBA，一种与LoRA兼容的适配器，通过用二进制符号和通道级缩放替代浮点值，将密集适配器分支转换为符号累加矩阵乘法。该方法在保持性能的同时将适配器占用空间减少10倍以上，预填充延迟开销最多仅8%。

<h3 id="214">[214] Spectral Probe-Circuits: A Three-Step Recipe for Identifying Attention-Head Circuits in Pretrained Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24059) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongzhong Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种三步方法来识别预训练Transformer中的注意力头电路：通过频谱信号排序、任务模式筛选和组消融验证。该方法在多种模型规模和架构中成功识别归纳电路，无需标签或归因梯度即可发现专门化计算的头。

<h3 id="215">[215] Federated Learning over Human-Body Communication for On-Body Edge Intelligence: A Survey, Taxonomy, and BODYFED-HBC Scheduling Vignette</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24062) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Koffka Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Edge Computing, Wearable Computing
- **Summary**: 该综述探讨了联邦学习与人体通信在可穿戴体域网络中的交叉应用。作者提出了区分体内、体域、跨用户和临床云FL部署的分类法，引入BODYFED-HBC参考架构，并识别了体域信道感知联邦学习的开放问题。

<h3 id="216">[216] Generative Representation Learning on Hyper-relational Knowledge Graphs via Masked Discrete Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaejun Lee, Seheon Kim, Joyce Jiyoung Whang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Diffusion Model, Knowledge Graph Completion
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出KREPE，首个用于超关系知识图谱的生成式表示学习方法，通过掩码离散扩散建模缺失组件的概率分布。该方法统一了链接预测和事实生成任务，在标准HKG链接预测基准上达到最先进性能，并在生成新颖正确事实方面优于LLM基线。

<h3 id="217">[217] When the Manual Lies: A Realistic Benchmark to Evaluate MCP Poisoning Attacks for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24069](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24069) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shi Liu, Xuehai Tang, Xikang Yang, Liang Lin, Biyu Zhou, Wenjie Xiao, Wantao Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文研究了针对LLM代理的工具描述中毒（TDP）攻击，即通过在工具描述元数据中注入恶意指令来影响代理的规划与决策。作者提出了MCP-TDP安全基准进行评估，发现主流LLM存在严重漏洞，并提出了一种“反应性自我纠正”的防御机制。

<h3 id="218">[218] Not All Transitions Matter: Evidence from PPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24071) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ajhesh Basnet
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文表明在PPO训练中随机丢弃部分转换可以打破重复梯度结构并稳定训练。通过在保持奖励信号完整性的阶段丢弃25%的转换，该方法在五个环境中匹配原始PPO的奖励性能，同时产生更一致的训练动态。

<h3 id="219">[219] TRACER: A Semantic-Aware Framework for Fine-Grained Contamination Detection in Code LLMs</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Benchmark Contamination, LLM Evaluation, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了TRACER框架，用于检测代码大语言模型中的细粒度数据污染问题，通过三级语义重叠建模和由粗到细的检测管道，在二分类设置下F1达到0.92，比现有方法提升42%-217%。

<h3 id="220">[220] Verified SHAP: Provable Bounds for Exact Shapley Values of Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24084) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO

<h3 id="221">[221] The Time is Here for Just-in-Time Systems: Challenges and Opportunities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24096) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, System Design
- **Summary**: 本文提出了即时系统（Just-in-Time Systems）的概念，利用LLM编码代理从零开始合成专门化的系统，在键值存储合成任务上击败了18个规格中的所有SOTA系统，最高提升4.6倍。

<h3 id="222">[222] Overcoming "Physics Shock" in Earth Observation A Heteroscedastic Uncertainty Framework for PINN-based Flood Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24106) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种针对地球观测的不确定性感知PINN框架，通过异方差不确定性建模解决物理信息神经网络在SAR洪水推断中的梯度发散问题，IoU相对提升25%。

<h3 id="223">[223] MASt3R-Nav: WayPixel Navigation in Relative 3D Maps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24111) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vansh Garg, Rohit Jayanti, Krish Pandya, Sarthak Chittawar, Siddharth Tourani, Muhammad Haris Khan, Sourav Garg, Madhava Krishna
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Navigation, SLAM, Robotics

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于像素相对连通性的新型地图表示方法，无需全局几何一致性即可实现精确的视觉导航，在仿真和真实世界中验证了四种导航任务的有效性。

<h3 id="224">[224] Empirical Analysis and Detection of Hallucinations in LLM-Generated Bug Report Summaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24137) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="225">[225] Understanding Conversational Patterns in Multi-agent Programming: A Case Study on Fibonacci Game Development</h3>

<h3 id="226">[226] An Interpretable CF-RL-TOPSIS Fusion Model for Skills-Aware Talent Recommendation</h3>

<h3 id="227">[227] Knowledge Graph Modulated Deep Learning for Limited-Sample Clinical Data Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24162](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24162) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="228">[228] PromptAudit: Auditing Prompt Sensitivity in LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection</h3>

<h3 id="229">[229] Extracting Training Data from Diffusion Language Models via Infilling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24173) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG

<h3 id="230">[230] Human-AI Collaboration in Science at Scale: A Global Large-scale Randomized Field Experiment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24180](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24180) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="231">[231] AvalancheBench: Evaluating Enterprise Data Agents Through Latent World Recovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24183) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="232">[232] Filtered Posterior Mean Collections: A Unified Framework for Analytical Models of Diffusion Generalization</h3>

<h3 id="233">[233] Teaching Through Analogies: A Modular Pipeline for Educational Analogy Generation</h3>

- **Authors**: Mariam Barakat, Ekaterina Kochmar
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="234">[234] Distributionally Robust Transfer Learning with Structurally Missing Covariates, with Application to Cross-National Cardiac Arrest Prediction</h3>

- **Subjects**: stat.AP; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML

<h3 id="235">[235] Towards Evaluation Engineering: An Empirical Study of ML Evaluation Harnesses in the Wild</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文对57个评估工具进行实证研究，建立五阶段模型并分类16560个问题，发现41.4%的挑战集中在规范阶段，未实现功能、文档缺失和输入验证缺失是三大根本原因。

<h3 id="236">[236] Agent-ToM: Learning to Monitor Autonomous LLM Agents via Theory-of-Mind Reasoning</h3>

- **Authors**: Nesreen K. Ahmed, Nima Nafisi

<h3 id="237">[237] Unlocking Apple's Private Cloud Compute: An Analysis of Privacy-Preserving Artificial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24239](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24239) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yannik Dittmar, Marvin Jerome Stephan, Thomas Völkl, Matthias Hollick, Jiska Classen
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

<h3 id="238">[238] GIBLy: Improving 3D Semantic Segmentation through an Architecture-Agnostic Lightweight Geometric Inductive Bias Layer</h3>

<h3 id="239">[239] Improving Labeling Consistency with Detailed Constitutional Definitions and AI-Driven Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="240">[240] Attested Tool-Server Admission: A Security Extension to the Model Context Protocol</h3>

<h3 id="241">[241] CRISP -- Clustering-Based Redundancy-Reduced Instance Sampling for Pathology Case Representation and Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24253) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="242">[242] An Interactive Paradigm for Deep Research</h3>

<h3 id="243">[243] Concept Drift Adaptation Using Self-Supervised and Reinforcement Learning In Android Malware Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24294](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24294) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="244">[244] Benchmarking Patent Embeddings: A Multi-Task Evaluation of 22 Models Across Retrieval, Classification, and Clustering</h3>

<h3 id="245">[245] An Empirical Evaluation of LLM-Generated Code Security Across Prompting Methods</h3>

<h3 id="246">[246] Enhancing Reliability in LLM-Based Secure Code Generation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Security, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了缓解感知链式思维(MA-CoT)框架，通过嵌入CWE缓解指导和语言感知保障来减少生成代码中的漏洞。实验表明MA-CoT在主数据集上将安全发现总数减少57.6%，在LLMSecEval上减少94.5%，是唯一持续改善安全可靠性的策略。

<h3 id="247">[247] ArtSplat: Feed-Forward Articulated 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Multi-State Uncalibrated Views</h3>

<h3 id="248">[248] ChaosBench-Logic v2: Evaluating LLM Logical Reasoning over Dynamical Systems at Scale</h3>

<h3 id="249">[249] ScaleAcross Explorer: Exploring Communication Optimization for Scale-Across AI Model Training</h3>

<h3 id="256">[256] Batch Normalization Amplifies Memorization and Privacy Risks</h3>

<h3 id="257">[257] Momentum Streams for Optimizer-Inspired Transformers</h3>

<h3 id="258">[258] Temporal Concept Drift in Legal Judgment Prediction: Neural Baselines Across Three Epochs of Ukrainian Court Decisions</h3>

<h3 id="259">[259] Code2UML: Agentic LLMs with context engineering for scalable software visualization</h3>

<h3 id="260">[260] Balancing Fairness, Privacy, and Accuracy: A Multitask Adversarial Framework for Centralized Data-Driven Systems</h3>

<h3 id="261">[261] Coarse-to-Fine Domain Incremental Learning with Attentive Distillation for Mining Footprint Segmentation in Multispectral Imagery</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Action Prediction
- **Summary**: 本文提出了TRAFA系统，一种用于程序任务的实时预测反馈系统，可在错误发生前进行干预。该系统采用Track-Forecast-Act框架跟踪手部和物体状态、预测用户动作，并在预测动作可能违反任务约束时触发反馈，有效提升了任务准确性和效率。

<h3 id="268">[268] Is Decentralized AI Governable? From Regulative Policy to Constitutive Protocol</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24538](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24538) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Botao Amber Hu, Helena Rong
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文分析了去中心化AI的治理挑战，指出其导致"治理真空"——系统足够重要需要治理但缺乏现有治理框架所预设的可识别责任主体。文章主张从政策治理转向协议治理，通过架构约束而非规范性指导来实现治理。

<h3 id="269">[269] SemanticZip: A Pilot Framework for Lossy Text Compression with LLMs as Semantic Decompressors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24541](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24541) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Natalia Trukhina, Vadim Vashkelis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Text Compression
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SemanticZip框架，一种以LLM作为语义解压缩器的有损文本压缩方法。该框架将文本压缩为紧凑代码，由LLM展开为任务相关的语义内容，在六种表示机制上评估发现，结构化散文具有最高可恢复性，SemanticZip ASCII提供最大有用压缩。

<h3 id="270">[270] AI-Driven Adaptive Adversaries and the Erosion of Cryptographic Trust in Public Key Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24542) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Petar Radanliev
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, AI Security
- **Summary**: 本文研究了AI驱动的自适应对抗者如何通过利用实现层面的可观察性而非破解密码原语来侵蚀公钥密码学的安全性。文章指出了算法中心的密码安全模型与实际攻击现实之间日益增长的不匹配问题。

<h3 id="271">[271] Rethinking Federated Unlearning via the Lens of Memorization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24545](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24545) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaheng Wei, Yanjun Zhang, He Zhang, Leo Yu Zhang, Chao Chen, Kok-Leong Ong, Jun Zhang, Yang Xiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Machine Unlearning
- **Venue**: SIGKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 本文从记忆化视角重新审视联邦遗忘学习，提出了联邦记忆剪枝(FedMemPrune)方法。该方法通过剪枝负责记忆化的冗余参数来移除待遗忘数据的独特记忆信息，同时保留与剩余数据重叠的模式，在不牺牲保留知识效用的情况下实现了强遗忘性能。

<h3 id="272">[272] PEDESTRIANQA: A Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Pedestrian Intention and Trajectory Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24562](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24562) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Naman Mishra, Shankar Gangisetty, C. V. Jawahar
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Vision-Language Model, Trajectory Prediction
- **Summary**: 本文引入PedestrianQA基准，将行人意图和轨迹预测表述为带有结构化理由的问答任务。该大规模视频数据集使视觉语言模型能够从视觉动态和上下文线索中学习，在PIE、JAAD、TITAN和IDD-PeD数据集上微调后显著提升了意图分类、轨迹预测和解释质量。

<h3 id="273">[273] PILOT: Policy-Informed Learned Optimization for Adaptive Deep Network Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24570](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24570) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sattam Altuuaim, Lama Ayash, Muhammad Mubashar, Naeemullah Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SattamAltwaim/PILOT.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PILOT，一种在线优化器，通过使用梯度方向一致性作为训练稳定性信号来动态调整更新行为。实验表明，PILOT在FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的一致性准确率最高，在ResNet-18上分别达到95.71%和93.42%。

<h3 id="274">[274] Polymorphism Is Rotation: Operational Mechanistic Interpretability from a Two-Layer Transformer to Pythia-70m</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24577](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24577) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jordan F. McCann
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文发现独立训练的transformer在残差流基上通过均匀随机旋转计算相同函数，称之为多态现象。作者证明单个正交Procrustes旋转可以在模型间迁移稀疏自编码器特征字典和引导向量，无需重新训练。

<h3 id="275">[275] LAPLEX: The FFT of Learnable Laplace Kernels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24584) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Łukasz Struski, Hanna Blazhko, Piotr Kubaty, Jacek Tabor
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Efficient Architecture, Representation Learning, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了LAPLEX，一类可精确计算的可训练拉普拉斯核算子，具有类似FFT的扩展性，支持在GPU上处理高达10^9维向量的可训练矩阵-向量运算，实现了在不存储密集矩阵的情况下学习密集几何结构。

<h3 id="276">[276] Correcting Visual Blur Induced by Attention Distraction to Reduce Hallucinations: Algorithm and Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24602) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quanjiang Li, Zhiming Liu, Wei Luo, Tingjin Luo, Chenping Hou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了多模态大语言模型中的幻觉与注意力分散现象密切相关，表现为多头注意力的空间不一致性和解码过程中对图像标记注意力的时间衰减。作者提出AFIP方法，通过跨头注意力增强和动态历史注意力增强来纠正注意力分散。

<h3 id="277">[277] Catching MRI outliers: unsupervised detection and localization of MRI artefacts and clinical anomalies using deep learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mustafa Kadhim, Viktor Rogowski, Emilia Persson, Camila Gonzalez, André Haraldsson, Sofie Ceberg, Mikael Nilsson, Malin Kügele, Sven Bäck, Christian Jamtheim Gustafsson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个全自动无监督异常检测框架，用于盆腔和脑部MRI的质量控制。该框架结合感知图像差异和标记惊讶度分数，在盆腔MRI上达到0.97的AUC，在脑部MRI上达到0.81的AUC。

<h3 id="278">[278] Guarded Repair for Harm-Aware Post-hoc Replacement of LLM Mathematical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haizhou Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Haizhoux0517/guarded-repair)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GuardedRepair框架，用于选择性修复LLM数学推理轨迹，结合轻量级符号检查、语义风险诊断和保守接受策略。在GSM8K上，该方法将准确率从95.60%提升至96.89%，且不会破坏原本正确的答案。

<h3 id="279">[279] Measuring the Depth of LLM Unlearning via Activation Patching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24614](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24614) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaeung Lee, Dohyun Kim, Jaemin Jo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Evaluation, Interpretability
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gnueaj/unlearning-depth-score)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了遗忘深度分数(UDS)指标，通过激活修补量化LLM遗忘的机制深度，解决了现有输出级指标无法检测内部表示中残留知识的问题，在150个遗忘模型上的元评估中验证了其高保真度和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="280">[280] Phase-Aware Wavelet-Based-Scattering Encoder-Decoder for Dense Predictions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24621) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ghassen Marrakchi, Basarab Matei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了遗忘深度分数(UDS)，一种通过激活修补量化LLM遗忘机制深度的指标。在150个遗忘模型的元评估中，UDS在忠实度和鲁棒性方面表现最佳，为遗忘评估提供了可靠的因果方法。

<h3 id="281">[281] Beyond Generative Priors: Minority Sampling with JEPA-Guided Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sol Park, Soobin Um
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Image Restoration, Image Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/soobin-um/jepa-guidance)
- **Summary**: 本文提出相位感知散射编解码器，通过在跳跃连接中显式保留相位信息来恢复密集预测任务所需的空间结构。在图像去噪任务上，打破平移不变性提升PSNR 2.17dB，相位保留额外增加1.03dB。

<h3 id="282">[282] Demystifying the Mythos or Disrupting Bugonomics? From Zero-Day Asymmetry to Defender Remediation Throughput</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alfredo Pesoli, Herman Errico, Lorenzo Cavallaro
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vulnerability Detection, Cybersecurity, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文从漏洞经济学视角审视LLM驱动的漏洞发现，论证近期趋势并非简单增加零日漏洞数量，而是推动防御方补救吞吐量的提升，使低信号候选更廉价、证据丰富的补救更重要，瓶颈转向维护者审查和发布工作。

<h3 id="283">[283] DisDop: Distillation with Domain Priors for Open-Vocabulary Aerial Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruihao Xu, Yong Liu, Yansong Tang, Sule Bai, Xubing Ye, Bingyao Yu, Yutao Guo, Jiwen Lu, Jie Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Diffusion Sampling
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了JEPA引导的扩散采样框架，用于生成与真实世界语义稀有性对齐的低密度样本。该方法在无条件、类条件和文本到图像生成任务中均优于生成器中心基线，更好地捕捉真实世界的稀有性概念。

<h3 id="284">[284] On the Stability and Realizability of Recurrent Polynomial Surrogate Ternary Logic Gate Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24649) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sai Sandeep Damera, Ryan Matheu, Aniruddh G. Puranic, John S. Baras, Calin Belta
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Knowledge Distillation, Remote Sensing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DisDop框架，系统地将遥感基础模型的多级领域先验蒸馏到轻量级检测器中，用于开放词汇航空目标检测。该方法结合RemoteCLIP的跨模态对齐能力和DINOv3的细粒度特征提取能力，实现了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="285">[285] How Many Tools Should an LLM Agent See? A Chance-Corrected Answer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24660) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vyzantinos Repantis, Ameya Gawde, Harshvardhan Singh, Joey Blackwell II
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文引入R-DTLGN，一种在Kleene三值逻辑上运行的循环架构，为安全关键系统的运行时监视器提供形式化的降级保证。该网络通过连续多项式代理训练，在推理时硬化为离散三值逻辑电路。

<h3 id="286">[286] CyBOKClaw: Human-in-the-Loop CyBOK Mapping for Cybersecurity Curriculum</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24663](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24663) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yan Lin Aung, Kevin Togbe
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文将LLM代理看到的工具数量作为评估对象，应用机会校正指标BoR来评估给定深度是否优于随机选择。作者开发了RL代理来学习每个查询的工具短列表深度，在保持覆盖率的同时显著减少平均展示工具数。

<h3 id="287">[287] VaaWIT: Visual-Aware Adaptation of Large Language Models for Multilingual Web Image Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24675](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24675) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bo Li, Ronghao Chen, Ningyuan Deng, Huacan Wang, Shaolin Zhu, Lijie Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CyBOKClaw，一个可解释的人在环检索框架，用于将网络安全关键词映射到网络安全知识体系。该框架结合查询规范化、术语扩展、概念级增强和领域敏感排序规则，在验证集上达到98%的专家指导有用性。

<h3 id="288">[288] Mix-MoE: Improving Multilingual Machine Translation of Large Language Models through Mixed MoEs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24681) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bo Li, Tianyu Dong, Shaolin Zhu, Deyi Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了VaaWIT端到端框架，通过双流注意力模块和视觉感知适配器将LLM适配于多语言网页图像翻译。该方法在三个公共基准的八个任务上显著优于最先进的开源基线，并与专有模型取得竞争性能。

<h3 id="289">[289] Beyond the Aggregation Dilemma: Prior-Retaining Decoupled Learning for Multimodal Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24684) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Yan, Xuanru Wang, Jun Yin, Shirui Pan, Senzhang Wang, Chengqi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Graph Neural Network, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多模态属性图学习中使用大型基础模型时出现的聚合困境问题，提出了一种解耦双路径架构SUPRA。该方法通过拓扑无关的MLP处理模态特定特征，同时通过轻量级共享GNN捕获结构协同效应，有效解决了表示病理和优化病理问题。

<h3 id="290">[290] HoloFair: Unified T2I Fairness Evaluation and Fair-GRPO Debiasing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24687) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruyi Chen, Lu Zhou, Xiaogang Xu, Chiyu Zhang, Jiafei Wu, Liming Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Fairness, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/1059684669/HoloFair)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了HoloFair，一个用于文本到图像模型多维人口统计偏差分析的综合基准框架，以及Fair-GRPO强化学习去偏方法。实验表明Fair-GRPO在保持高图像质量的同时显著提高了多维公平性。

<h3 id="291">[291] The Path Matters: Learning a Token-Commitment Policy for Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24697) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bohang Sun, Max Zhu, Francesco Caso, Jindong Gu, Junchi Yu, Philip Torr, Pietro Liò, Jialin Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text Generation, Decoding Strategy
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/BobSun98/TraceLock)
- **Summary**: 本文针对扩散语言模型的token提交决策问题，提出了TraceLock轻量级插件控制器，通过从未来稳定性中获取自监督来学习可复用的轨迹状态策略，在问答、数学推理和代码生成任务上改善了质量-步骤权衡。

<h3 id="292">[292] TS-Skill: A Benchmark for Evaluating Analytical Skills in Time-Series Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24703) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liying Han, Kang Yang, Oliver Wang, Jason Wu, Pengrui Quan, Gaofeng Dong, Ozan Baris Mulayim, Sizhe Ma, Yuyang Yuan, Dezhi Hong, Mario Berges, Mani Srivastava
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Question Answering, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文介绍了TS-Skill，一个用于评估时间序列问答中三种可组合分析技能的基准：时间尺度选择、时间定位和跨区间整合。作者开发了SKEvol技能引导代理框架来构建基准。

<h3 id="293">[293] World-State Transformations for Neuro-symbolic Interactive Storytelling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24719](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24719) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Santiago Góngora, Luis Chiruzzo, Gonzalo Méndez, Pablo Gervás
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Interactive Storytelling, LLM Application
- **Venue**: ICCC 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文探索了使用LLM预测基于规则的交互式叙事系统中的状态变化，触发预编程的世界状态转换。实验表明这种神经符号方法在保持世界状态一致性的同时鼓励玩家创造性交互。

<h3 id="294">[294] Who judges the judges? Governance from metrics: a runtime framework for continuous LLM compliance monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24737](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24737) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Governance, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 该论文介绍了govllm，一个用于持续LLM合规性监控的开源框架，使用运行时可观测性。该框架使用监管法官小组（LLM评估器）并实现基于合规性分数的治理驱动路由。

<h3 id="295">[295] Bilevel Optimization of Synthetic Trajectories for Multi-Turn LLM Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24743) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了BOOST，一个用于多轮LLM微调的双层优化框架，学习重新加权合成轨迹。内层在重新加权的数据上训练LLM，外层在保留的真实验证任务上训练轻量级重加权头。

<h3 id="296">[296] Motion-Compensated Weight Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24754) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Model Compression, Neural Compression, Quantization

<h3 id="297">[297] Spectral Retrieval: Multi-Scale Sinc Convolution over Token Embeddings for Localized Retrieval in LLM Multi-Agent Systems</h3>

<h3 id="298">[298] Leveraging pretrained RGB denoisers for hyperspectral image restoration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24769) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniele Picone, Mohamad Jouni, Mauro Dalla-Mura
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Hyperspectral Imaging, Transfer Learning, Image Restoration
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种最小训练的轻量级适配器，通过投影映射将冻结的预训练RGB去噪器重新用于高光谱图像恢复。该方法对低维光谱投影进行去噪，并通过约束线性聚合重建高光谱立方体。

<h3 id="299">[299] From Theory to Decision Rule: Calibrating the Noisy-Label Crossover for Vision-Language Model Weak Supervision Across Three Medical-Imaging Benchmarks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24771) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bruce Changlong Xu, Jose James, Alexander Ryu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Weak Supervision, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 该论文为三个医学影像基准上的BiomedCLIP生成弱标签提供了校准，识别弱标签何时停止帮助并开始损害下游性能。作者推导出一个可从10-20个金标签操作的决策规则。

<h3 id="300">[300] Complement Submodular Information Measures for Balanced and Robust Data Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24779](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24779) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rishabh Iyer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.CO
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Submodular Optimization, Dataset
- **Summary**: 该论文介绍了互补子模态信息（CSI），一类新的互补感知子模态目标，量化子集与其补集之间的共享结构信息。该框架在保持稀有/尾部语义结构的同时抑制噪声异常值。

<h3 id="301">[301] CONF-KV: Confidence-Aware KV Cache Eviction with Mixed-Precision Storage for Long-Horizon LLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yubo Li, Yidi Miao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: 该论文介绍了CONF-KV，一种KV缓存管理器，使用模型不确定性动态调整缓存预算。该方法结合驱逐策略与混合精度存储，在较低内存下实现接近全KV的性能。

<h3 id="302">[302] Parameter-Efficient VLMs for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy: Medical Image Generation and Clinical Visual Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24792](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24792) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ojonugwa Oluwafemi Ejiga Peter, Frederick Akor Ejiga, Fahmi Khalifa, Md Mahmudur Rahman
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个双管道PEFT模型用于胃肠道内窥镜检查，解决医学VQA和隐私保护合成数据生成问题。Florence-2 VQA模型实现了强ROUGE分数，而带有Stable Diffusion的LoRA生成了高质量的GI图像。

<h3 id="303">[303] Divide-and-Conquer Inference for Large-Scale Visual Recognition with Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24799](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24799) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhipeng Ye, Jiaqi Huang, Feng Jiang, Qiufeng Wang, Yikang Duan, Dawei Wang, Xihang Zhou, Qian Qiao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Image Classification, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了分而治之推理（DCI），一种用于MLLM视觉识别的测试时缩放策略，递归地将复杂分类任务分解为更简单的子问题。该方法解决了由注意力稀释引起的长序列识别中的性能崩溃。

<h3 id="304">[304] Zero-Shot Parkinson's Disease Detection from Speech: Comparing Large Audio and Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24806) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Ashad Kabir, Sirajam Munira
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Zero-Shot Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 该论文系统地比较了零样本帕金森病检测的两种输入模态：由LLM分析的手工声学特征和由音频能力模型分析的原始波形输入。结果表明性能因模态、任务和语言而异。

<h3 id="305">[305] Disentangled Double Machine Learning for Accurate Causal Effect Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guodu Xiang, Kui Yu, Yujie Wang, Richang Hong, Fuyuan Cao, Jiye Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了解纠缠双重机器学习(DDML)算法，通过因果角色解纠缠策略将协变量分解为混淆因子、处理特定因子和结果特定因子，并采用残差依赖正交化策略来提高因果效应估计的精度。实验结果表明DDML在合成、半合成和真实数据集上显著优于13种基线算法。

<h3 id="306">[306] Cross-Domain Energy-Guided Diffusion Generation for Off-Dynamics Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24810) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Yang, Yihong Guo, Anqi Liu, Pan Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Diffusion Model, Trajectory Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出CEDGE框架，用于跨域离线强化学习，通过在源域轨迹上训练扩散模型并利用能量引导将生成样本适应到目标域，实现轨迹级别的生成而非传统的转移级别生成。实验表明该方法在动态偏移下的扩散规划和下游策略学习方面均有改进。

<h3 id="307">[307] Multiscale Real-Time Object Detection in the NMS-Free Era: A Comparative Performance Evaluation of YOLOv8 and YOLO26</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24831) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chidera G. Oguine, Kanyifeechukwu J. Oguine, Obiozor M. Oguine, Ozioma C. Oguine
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文比较了基于NMS的YOLOv8和无NMS设计的YOLO26在目标检测任务上的性能，在Pascal VOC和VisDrone数据集上进行了多尺度评估。结果显示YOLO26在Pascal VOC上表现更好，但在密集小目标检测的VisDrone上两者表现接近，说明检测器选择需考虑数据集特征和硬件约束。

<h3 id="308">[308] Reflect-Guard: Enhancing LLM Safeguards against Adversarial Prompts via Logical Self-Reflection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24834) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lixing Lin, Juli You, Yue Li, Luyun Lin, Yiqing Wang, Zhen Zhang, Moxuan Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Detection, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文提出Reflect-Guard方法，通过参数高效微调将链式思维自反思能力注入LLM安全分类器，使其能够识别对抗性越狱攻击。仅使用1000个训练样本和更新0.5%的模型参数，该方法在WildGuardTest上F1提升7.2个百分点，在JailbreakBench上攻击成功率降低82.5%。

<h3 id="309">[309] Adversarial Error Correction for Visual Autoregressive Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24843) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ligong Bi, Tao Huang, Jianyuan Guo, Chang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Autoregressive Model, Adversarial Robustness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bijiw515/AID-VAR)
- **Summary**: 本文提出AID-VAR框架，通过对抗性注入诊断增强预训练视觉自回归模型，使用判别器诊断尺度转换间的保真度差距，并引入轻量级引导注入器来修正特征流形。实验表明AID-VAR-d20在仅增加3%参数的情况下实现了16%的FID改进。

<h3 id="310">[310] Tiny Brains, Giant Impact: Uncovering the Keystone Neurons of LLM with Just a Few Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24846) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiangtian Ji, Yuxin Chen, Zhengzhou Cai, Xiang Wang, An Zhang, Tat-Seng Chua
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Fine-Tuning, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文发现在多种能力维度的任务中，LLM中存在一小部分神经元在推理过程中持续高度激活，称为关键神经元。移除这些神经元会导致模型行为崩溃，而仅更新这些神经元的监督微调方法可达到与全参数微调相当或更好的任务性能。

<h3 id="311">[311] The Concept Allocation Zone: Tracking How Concepts Form Across Transformer Depth</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24856) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: James Henry
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Transformer Architecture, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jamesrahenry/Rosetta_Tools)
- **Summary**: 本文引入概念分配区域(CAZ)框架来追踪概念在Transformer深度上的形成过程，通过三个层级指标(分离度、概念一致性、概念速度)形式化CAZ边界检测，在34个模型上的验证揭示了分离曲线的多模态特性。

<h3 id="312">[312] DBPnet: Damper Characteristics-Based Bayesian Physics-Informed Neural Network for Wheel Load Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24860](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24860) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Wang, Tianyi Zeng, Zimo Zeng, Feiyang Zhang, Yujin Wang, Xiangyu Li, Yiming Xu, Sikai Chen, Junfeng Jiao, Christian Claudel, Xinbo Chen
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Autonomous Driving, Bayesian Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出DBPnet，一种基于阻尼器特性的贝叶斯物理信息神经网络，用于车轮载荷估计。该方法结合悬架连杆级建模、贝叶斯推断和物理感知嵌入模块，在高保真仿真和真实实验中均表现出比基线方法更低的RMSE和最大误差。

<h3 id="313">[313] Towards a Universal Causal Reasoner</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24873) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qirun Dai, Xiao Liu, Jiawei Zhang, Dylan Zhang, Hao Peng, Chenhao Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Reasoning, LLM Reasoning, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出UniCo数据生成框架，覆盖Pearl因果阶梯的18种因果查询类型，并将符号示例转换为代码和自然语言形式。使用66.6K实例微调后，模型在18种分布内查询类型上平均提升22.9%，在7个分布外因果基准上提升8.1%，在医疗、法律和表格推理任务中展现出更忠实的推理轨迹。

<h3 id="314">[314] When Reasoning Hurts: Source-Aware Evaluation of Frontier LLMs for Clinical SOAP Note Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24902) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Faizan Faisal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Medical AI, RAG
- **Summary**: 本文评估了推理增强型LLM在临床SOAP笔记生成任务上的表现，发现启用推理功能反而会降低GPT模型在三个数据集上的性能，而同源RAG带来的改进较小且因模型而异。研究表明更强的推理能力不应被假设能自动改善对保真度敏感的临床文档生成任务。

<h3 id="315">[315] On the Impact of Class Imbalance on the Learning Dynamics of Deep Neural Networks:An Intuitive Insight</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24908) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ismail B. Mustapha, Shafaatunnur Hasan, Sunday O. Olatunji, Hatem S. Y. Nabus
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Class Imbalance, Deep Learning Theory, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了类别不平衡对DNN学习动态的影响，发现DNN在早期训练轮次中对少数类样本欠拟合而只学习多数类，最终学习的少数类表示因过拟合而缺乏泛化能力。

<h3 id="316">[316] Factorize to Generalize: Retrieval-Guided Invariant-Dynamic Decomposition for Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24911) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinjin Chi, Lei Feng, Lulu Zhang, Yongcheng Jing, Yiming Wang, Ximing Li, Jialie Shen, Leszek Rutkowski, Dacheng Tao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Class Imbalance, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了类别不平衡对深度神经网络学习动态的影响，发现类别不平衡会导致模型在早期训练轮次中欠拟合少数类样本而仅学习多数类，最终学习的少数类表示缺乏泛化能力，仅通过过拟合来保持较低的整体训练损失。

<h3 id="317">[317] Explainable Retinal Imaging for Prediction of Multi-Organ Dysfunction in Type 2 Diabetes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24912](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24912) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mini Han Wang, Liting Huang, Wei Hong, Boonthawan Wingwon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.OT
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, RAG, Distribution Shift
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MiniHanWang/Type-2-Diabetes-1.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出检索引导的不变量-动态分解框架用于时间序列预测，通过注意力聚合构建检索感知表示，并引入检索引导路由机制将其分解为不变分量和动态分量。该方法在分布偏移下提高了鲁棒性，在零样本预测设置中优于现有时间序列基础模型。

<h3 id="318">[318] Explainable Multi-Task Retinal Imaging Reveals Microvascular Signals for Systemic Risk Stratification in Type 2 Diabetes: A Pilot Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24913) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mini Han Wang, Liting Huang, Wei Hong, Boonthawan Wingwon
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Explainable AI, Feature Attribution
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个可解释的数据驱动框架，用于量化2型糖尿病患者的系统性多器官功能障碍风险。梯度提升模型实现了近乎完美的判别能力(AUC=1.000)，SHAP特征归因分析揭示了高血糖、肾功能损害、血脂异常和炎症是主要风险驱动因素。

<h3 id="319">[319] Quaternion Self-Attention with Shared Scores</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24920) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shogo Yamauchi, Tohru Nitta, Hideaki Tamori
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Speech Enhancement, Model Compression
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出共享分数四元数自注意力机制，通过四元数内积计算单一实值分数并在所有分量上共享注意力分布，将分数计算乘法减少75%，softmax操作从四个减少到一个。在语音增强任务中，GPU推理时间减少高达44.3%，同时保持质量。

<h3 id="320">[320] HumanEgo: Zero-Shot Robot Learning from Minutes of Human Egocentric Videos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24934) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wang, Botao He, Kelin Yu, Seungjae Lee, Ruohan Gao, Furong Huang, Yiannis Aloimonos
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Embodied AI, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出HumanEgo框架，通过将人类自我中心视频提升为手-物体交互的实体级表示，并使用密集辅助目标训练流匹配策略，实现零样本人机迁移。仅需每任务30分钟人类视频，该方法在四个真实世界任务上达到92.5%的平均成功率，比匹配时间的机器人遥操作高出41%。

<h3 id="321">[321] Your Embedding Model is SMARTer Than You Think</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24938) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianrui Zhang, Hyun Jung Lee, Sukanta Ganguly, Tae-Eui Kam, Donghyun Kim, Yong Jae Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal Retrieval, Late Interaction, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HanSolo9682/SMART)
- **Summary**: 本文提出SMART框架，通过利用标准单向量模型隐藏状态的潜在多向量能力，实现即插即用的多向量检索升级。该方法在多种模态上持续提升性能，无需额外训练即可让单向量模型超越最先进的多向量方法。

<h3 id="322">[322] Riemannian-Manifold Steering: Geometry-Aware Generative Autoencoders for Label-Free Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24942](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24942) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Narmeen Oozeer, Shivam Raval, Philip Quirke, Manikandan Ravikiran, Jeff Phillips, Shriyash Upadhyay, Amirali Abdullah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文将流形引导重新定义为激活空间上的黎曼测地线计算，提出了一种无需标签质心的无监督引导方法。通过学习编码器近似输出空间的Hellinger距离，该方法在语言模型算术基准测试中可靠地将模型引导至目标类别。

<h3 id="323">[323] RealBench: Benchmarking Data-Driven Numerical Weather Forecasting Under Operational Conditions and Extreme Event Challenges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24945](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24945) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruize Li, Zhibin Wen, Tao Han, Hao Chen, Fenghua Ling, Wei Zhang, Song Guo, Lei Bai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lixruize-del/NWP-Benchmark)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了RealBench基准，用于在真实操作条件下评估AI天气预报模型。该基准整合了低延迟操作分析和大规模全球实地观测数据，揭示了基于再分析的指标与真实性能之间的显著差异，特别是在极端事件方面。

<h3 id="324">[324] APT-Agent: Automated Penetration Testing using Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24949](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24949) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: William Guanting Li, Alsharif Abuadbba, Kristen Moore, Dan Dongseong Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Penetration Testing, LLM Agent, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 本文提出APT-Agent，一个完全自动化的LLM驱动渗透测试框架，通过混合纠正模块和命令特定记忆架构解决幻觉和长期上下文记忆问题。该系统在Metasploitable 2上实现了84.29%的端到端漏洞利用成功率。

<h3 id="325">[325] SEP-Attack: A Simple and Effective Paradigm for Transfer-Based Textual Adversarial Attack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24958](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24958) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han Liu, Zhi Xu, Xiaotong Zhang, Feng Zhang, Xiaoming Xu, Wei Wang, Fenglong Ma, Hong Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Text Classification
- **Summary**: 本文提出SEP-Attack，一种基于迁移的文本对抗攻击范式，使用行列式点过程生成多样化的代理集成权重来评估子模型的迁移性。该方法通过新的置信度评分指标和迁移性评分选择对抗样本，在四个数据集和两个真实API上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="326">[326] Investigating the Interplay between Contextual and Parametric Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness under Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24960](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24960) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingyi Sun, Qianli Wang, Pepa Atanasova, Nils Feldhus, Isabelle Augenstein
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, LLM Evaluation, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文研究了思维链(CoT)忠实性的两种评估范式——上下文忠实性和参数忠实性之间的关系，提出了FaithMate统一偏好对齐框架来优化模型。研究发现两种范式正相关但不完全对称，参数忠实性优化能带来更一致的跨范式提升。

<h3 id="327">[327] Cross-Domain Generalization Limits of Vision Foundation Models in Facial Deepfake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24965](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24965) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ibrahim Delibasoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, LLM Evaluation, Interpretability
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mribrahim/deepfake)
- **Summary**: 本文通过FaithMate统一偏好对齐接口研究思维链推理中上下文忠实性和参数忠实性的相互作用。研究发现两种范式正向耦合但不对称：优化参数忠实性能在两种范式中产生一致增益，而上下文对应方法的效果更不稳定。

<h3 id="328">[328] OSDTW: Optimal Shared Depth and Task Weighting for Long-Tailed Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24969) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chang Chu, Qingyue Zhang, Shao-Lun Huang, Junxiong Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long-Tailed Learning, Multi-Task Learning
- **Venue**: ICIC 2026 Oral
- **Summary**: 本文提出OSDTW，一个有原则的长尾识别任务分解框架，将问题分解为头部任务和尾部任务，使用共享编码器和任务特定解码器。该方法推导出可计算的偏差-方差分解来指导共享深度和任务权重的超参数选择。

<h3 id="329">[329] TGFormer: Towards Temporal Graph Transformer with Auto-Correlation Mechanism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24971) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="330">[330] MinerU-Popo: Universal Post-Processing Model for Structured Document Parsing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24973) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="331">[331] Bridging the Gap: Enabling Soft Actor Critic for High Performance Legged Locomotion</h3>

<h3 id="332">[332] Selective Test-Time Compute Scaling for Click-Through Rate Prediction via Uncertainty-Triggered Feature Path Exploration</h3>

<h3 id="333">[333] Scaling up Energy-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Mission-Oriented Drone Networks with Individual Reward</h3>

<h3 id="334">[334] Interpretation, Learning, and Empathy as One Constraint: A Residual-Adequacy Architecture with Accountable Abstention</h3>

<h3 id="335">[335] Metropolis-Scale Resilient and Trustworthy Traffic Flow Inference Using Multi-Source Data</h3>

<h3 id="336">[336] Performance Comparison of Classical and Neural Sampling Algorithms for Robotic Navigation</h3>

<h3 id="337">[337] D3S2: Diffusion-Guided Dataset Distillation for Semantic Segmentation</h3>

<h3 id="338">[338] Language Bias in LVLMs: From In-Depth Analysis to Simple and Effective Mitigation</h3>

<h3 id="340">[340] Scale When Needed: Adaptive Neuron-level Mixed Precision Quantization Aware Training</h3>

<h3 id="344">[344] Security in the Fine-Tuning Lifecycle of Large Language Models: Threats, Defenses,Evaluation, and Future Directions</h3>

<h3 id="349">[349] Courant: a State-Adaptive Perceiver-Based Neural Surrogate with Local Support and Interpretable Field Decomposition</h3>

<h3 id="350">[350] Trust-Aware Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy for Robust Domain Adaptation</h3>

<h3 id="352">[352] Inference-Time Alignment of Diffusion Models via Trust-Region Iterative Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo</h3>

<h3 id="353">[353] Theoretical Analysis of Sparse Optimization with Reparameterization, Weight Decay, and Adaptive Learning Rate</h3>

<h3 id="354">[354] ASTRO: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Reinforcement Optimization for GNN Powered Anomly Detection in Cyber Physical Systems</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了ASTRO框架，结合图神经网络、时序建模、多头注意力机制和深度Q网络，用于工业物联网环境中的异常检测。该方法通过强化学习动态优化阈值，在SWaT和WADI数据集上取得了优异性能。

<h3 id="355">[355] LLM Agent Based Renewable Energy Forecasting Using Edge and IoT Data A Review of Solar Wind Weather and Grid Aware Decision Support</h3>

<h3 id="356">[356] Abduction-Deduction Entanglement: Domain Generalization via Representation Transplants</h3>

<h3 id="357">[357] STREAM: A Data-Centric Framework for Mining High-Value Task-Oriented Dialogues from Streaming Media</h3>

<h3 id="358">[358] K-U-KAN: Koopman-Enhanced U-KAN for 3D Dental Reconstruction from a Single Panoramic X-ray Radiograph</h3>
