<h3 id="698">[698] Committed SAE-Feature Traces for Audited-Session Substitution Detection in Hosted LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18179) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Model Verification, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种commit-open协议，通过Merkle树承诺和SAE特征追踪来检测托管LLM中的模型替换攻击，能够有效识别各种攻击类型同时保持较低的额外开销。

<h3 id="699">[699] MoBayes: A Modular Bayesian Framework for Separating Reasoning from Language in Conversational Clinical Decision Support</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.20022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20022) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yusuf Kesmen, Fay Elhassan, Jiayi Ma, Julien Stalhandske, Yena Chang, David Sasu, Alexandra Kulinkina, Akhil Arora, Lars Klein, Mary-Anne Hartley
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Decision Support System, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了MoBayes模块化贝叶斯对话框架，将概率推理与语言生成分离。LLM仅作为语言接口解析患者对话为结构化观察，贝叶斯模块执行概率推理、选择后续问题并确定何时停止或转诊，在临床决策支持任务上超越了独立的前沿LLM医生。

<h3 id="700">[700] Human-1 by Josh Talks: A Full-Duplex Conversational Modeling Framework in Hindi using Real-World Conversations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23295) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bhaskar Singh, Shobhit Banga, Mahima Manik, Pranav Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Speech Processing, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了首个印地语全双工口语对话系统，通过适配Moshi架构并使用自定义印地语分词器，在26,000小时真实对话数据上训练。该系统能够建模自然对话行为如打断、重叠和反向通道，为印地语和其他印度语言的实时双工口语对话系统奠定了基础。

<h3 id="701">[701] Talking Slide Avatars: Open-Source Multimodal Communication Approach for Teaching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23703) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinxing Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Speech Synthesis, Video Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个开源的说话幻灯片头像制作工作流，集成OpenVoice文本转语音和Ditto-TalkingHead音频驱动说话图像合成。研究将说话幻灯片头像定位为数字教学法、审美教育和艺术技术实践交叉的多模态传播制品，并提供了脚本长度、图像选择、节奏和伦理使用的实用指南。

<h3 id="702">[702] ESIA: An Energy-Based Spatiotemporal Interaction-Aware Framework for Pedestrian Intention Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23728) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanping Wu, Meiting Dang, Lin Wu, Edmond S. L. Ho, Zhenghua Chen, Chongfeng Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Behavior Prediction, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了ESIA框架，将行人意图预测建模为基于条件随机场的结构化预测问题。该方法将行人和环境表示为时空节点，通过一元势捕获个体意图、成对势编码社会和环境交互，并使用Unary-Seeded Simulated Annealing算法高效求解，在标准基准上取得了最先进性能。

<h3 id="703">[703] Chain of Evidence: Pixel-Level Visual Attribution for Iterative Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01284](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01284) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Peiyang Liu, Ziqiang Cui, Xi Wang, Di Liang, Wei Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PeiYangLiu/CoE.git)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Chain of Evidence (CoE)视觉归因框架，用于迭代检索增强生成。该方法利用视觉语言模型直接对检索到的文档截图进行推理，输出精确的边界框，解决了粗粒度归因和视觉语义丢失两个关键瓶颈问题。

<h3 id="704">[704] VILAS: A VLA-Integrated Low-cost Architecture with Soft Grasping for Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02037) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zijian An, Hadi Khezam, Bill Cai, Ran Yang, Shijie Geng, Yiming Feng, Yue Zheng, Lifeng Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了VILAS低成本模块化机器人操作平台，支持端到端视觉-语言-动作策略学习和部署。系统集成了协作臂、电动夹爪和折纸式软顺应夹爪扩展，通过ZMQ通信架构协调遥操作、数据收集和策略部署，成功在葡萄抓取任务上验证了三个VLA模型的有效性。

<h3 id="705">[705] Soft-to-Hard Routing in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02124](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02124) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Reza Rastegar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.PR
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文研究了稀疏专家混合模型中软路由向硬路由逼近的数学性质，提出了边界层微积分方法来分析温度趋近零时的极限行为。研究发现零温度近似由路由接口的O(τ)邻域概率质量控制，而非仅由温度决定。

<h3 id="706">[706] Efficient Preference Poisoning Attack on Offline RLHF</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: RLHF, Data Poisoning, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了离线RLHF中的偏好投毒攻击，发现翻转一个偏好标签会导致DPO梯度的参数无关偏移。作者提出了BAL-A和BMP-A两种攻击方法，将目标投毒问题转化为结构化二元稀疏近似问题，并在理论和实验上验证了攻击有效性。

<h3 id="707">[707] Safety in Embodied AI: A Survey of Risks, Attacks, and Defenses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02900](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02900) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.RO

<h3 id="708">[708] From Muscle Bursts to Motor Intent: Self-Supervised Token Modeling for Heterogeneous EMG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03462) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenghao Huang, Huilin Yao, Kaikai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Biosignal Processing, Gesture Recognition
- **Summary**: 本文提出AEMG方法，通过自监督学习从异构EMG信号中提取可复用的神经肌肉表征。该方法将收缩事件表示为紧凑的神经肌肉token，并使用时空条件Transformer编码token序列，在跨用户和低标签适应场景下显著提升手势识别性能。

<h3 id="709">[709] Auditing Stealth Sycophancy in Mental-Health Dialogue: Structured Clinical-State Diagnostics and Clean Matched Benchmarks</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出BFORE框架，利用蝴蝶优化算法和萤火虫算法自动寻找最佳Retinex增强参数。该方法分两阶段优化多尺度Retinex参数和伽马校正、去噪、颜色参数，在合成和真实图像上均显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="710">[710] BFORE: Butterfly-Firefly Optimized Retinex Enhancement for Low-Light Image Quality Improvement</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出BFORE框架，结合蝴蝶优化算法和萤火虫算法自动寻找最佳的低光照图像增强参数，在合成和真实图像数据集上均显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="711">[711] LLMs Uncertainty Quantification via Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy</h3>

- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Hallucination, Conformal Prediction

<h3 id="712">[712] Mitigating Label Shift in Tabular In-Context Learning via Test-Time Posterior Adjustment</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Tabular Learning, Distribution Shift

- **Summary**: 本文发现TabPFN对标签偏移敏感，会过拟合训练数据中的多数类。作者提出DistPFN方法，通过测试时后验调整重新缩放预测类别概率，在保持标准设置性能的同时显著改善标签偏移场景下的分类效果。

<h3 id="713">[713] Sparse Tokens Suffice: Jailbreaking Audio Language Models via Token-Aware Gradient Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.04700](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04700) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.SD

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文发现音频语言模型中token对齐的梯度能量高度不均匀，只有小部分token主导优化信号。作者提出TAGO方法，通过保留高梯度能量token的稀疏优化实现高效越狱攻击，在保持攻击成功率的同时大幅减少计算量。

<h3 id="714">[714] Aes3D: Aesthetic Assessment in 3D Gaussian Splatting</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出Aes3D框架，首个用于3D高斯泼溅场景美学评估的系统方法。该方法构建了首个3D场景美学数据集Aesthetic3D，并设计了轻量级模型Aes3DGSNet直接从3D高斯基元预测场景级美学分数。

<h3 id="715">[715] Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fei Ding, Yongkang Zhang, Runhao Liu, Yuhao Liao, Zijian Zeng, Sibo wang, Huiming Yang

- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Process Supervision
- **Summary**: 本文提出将结果监督内化为过程监督的新范式，通过识别、纠正和复用失败推理轨迹自动提取过程级学习信号。该方法在仅有结果监督的情况下实现更细粒度的策略优化，为推理任务的强化学习开辟了新路径。

<h3 id="716">[716] E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qingjun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Model Training
- **Summary**: 本文引入无量纲控制参数E=T*H/(O+B)来预测MoE模型是否会发展健康的专家生态或崩溃为死专家。通过11000多个训练周期的实验证明，E\>=0.5足以保证零死专家，无需手工负载均衡辅助损失。

<h3 id="717">[717] PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Privacy, Fine-Tuning, Differential Privacy
- **Summary**: 本文提出PACZero系列方法，实现零互信息下的PAC私有LLM微调。通过符号量化子集聚合零阶梯度创造一致性步骤，在这些步骤发布符号不消耗条件互信息，在高隐私机制下获得可用效用。

<h3 id="718">[718] Quality-Conditioned Agreement in Automated Short Answer Scoring: Mid-Range Degradation and the Impact of Task-Specific Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07647) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Abigail Victoria Gurin Schleifer, Moriah Ariely, Beata Beigman Klebanov, Asaf Salman, Giora Alexandron
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Grading, LLM Evaluation, Education Technology
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文研究自动简答题评分中的质量条件一致性，发现所有AI模型在完全正确和完全错误回复上表现良好，但在中等范围回复上显著退化。任务特定适应程度越高，中等范围退化越轻，微调编码器模型表现最佳。

<h3 id="719">[719] Intelligent Truck Matching in Full Truckload Shipments using Ping2Hex approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07733) (replaced)

- **Venue**: iSCSi 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出ITM 2.0系统，将卡车与货物匹配建模为概率排序问题。该方法利用Uber H3六边形空间索引离散化GPS数据构建路线相似性特征，结合LightGBM梯度提升，在北美和欧洲分别实现26和14个百分点的精度提升。

<h3 id="720">[720] Flow-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Flow Matching Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08063) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation, Text-to-Image
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CostaliyA/Flow-OPD)
- **Summary**: 本文提出Flow-OPD框架，首次将在线策略蒸馏整合到流匹配模型中用于文本到图像对齐。该方法通过两阶段策略培养领域专业教师模型，再通过在线采样和密集轨迹级监督将异构专业知识整合到单一学生模型中。

<h3 id="721">[721] FactoryNet: A Large-Scale Dataset toward Industrial Time-Series Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09081) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Anomaly Detection, Foundation Model, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了FactoryNet，首个工业时间序列数据的通用预训练语料库，包含5100万数据点，涵盖6种实体的23000次任务执行。该数据集采用S-E-F-C统一模式，支持零样本跨实体迁移和高效的异常检测。

<h3 id="722">[722] Memorize Theorems, Not Instances: Probing SFT Generalization through Mathematical Reasoning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文研究了监督微调(SFT)如何削弱推理泛化能力，提出Theorem-SFT方法，将监督目标从记忆表面相关性转向显式定理应用。该方法在MATH基准上提升8.8%，在GeoQA上提升20.27%。

<h3 id="723">[723] Real vs. Semi-Simulated: Rethinking Evaluation for Treatment Effect Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10430) (replaced)
- **Authors**: George Panagopoulos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Treatment Effect Estimation, Causal Inference, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了处理效应估计中方法研究与实际应用之间的评估差距，发现反事实指标无法可靠地恢复可观测指标偏好的估计器，且半模拟基准上的排名无法迁移到真实数据集。研究建议在评估中纳入可观测指标和真实数据验证。

<h3 id="724">[724] An Uncertainty-Aware Resilience Micro-Agent for Causal Observability in the Computing Continuum</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出AURORA框架，用于边缘计算环境中灰色故障的诊断和缓解。该框架整合自由能原理、因果do演算和局部因果状态图，通过双门控执行机制实现0%破坏性操作率和62%修复准确率。

<h3 id="725">[725] Break the Brake, Not the Wheel: Untargeted Jailbreak via Entropy Maximization</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出UJEM-KL方法，通过最大化决策token的熵来翻转拒绝结果，实现对视觉语言模型的无目标越狱攻击。该方法在白盒攻击成功率上具有竞争力，并显著提高了跨模型迁移性。

<h3 id="726">[726] PASA: A Principled Embedding-Space Watermarking Approach for LLM-Generated Text under Semantic-Invariant Attacks</h3>

<h3 id="727">[727] SURGE: Surrogate Gradient Adaptation in Binary Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10989](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10989) (replaced)

<h3 id="728">[728] HEPA: A Self-Supervised Horizon-Conditioned Event Predictive Architecture for Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11130](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11130) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文提出HEPA架构，通过联合嵌入预测架构进行自监督预训练，用于时间序列事件预测。该方法在11个领域的14个基准上优于PatchTST、iTransformer等领先架构，且调参数量和标注数据需求更少。

<h3 id="729">[729] Fill the GAP: A Granular Alignment Paradigm for Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="730">[730] Persona-Model Collapse in Emergent Misalignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12850](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12850) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Davi Bastos Costa, Renato Vicente
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Fine-Tuning, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 本文提出涌现错位涉及人格模型崩溃的假设，即模型内部模拟、区分和维护一致角色的能力退化。通过道德敏感性和道德鲁棒性指标，发现不安全微调导致四个前沿模型的区分能力显著失调。

<h3 id="731">[731] Data Difficulty and the Generalization--Extrapolation Tradeoff in LLM Fine-Tuning</h3>

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了LLM微调中数据难度的作用，发现最优难度取决于数据集大小，且随数据预算增加向更难数据偏移。研究揭示了泛化差距与外推差距之间的权衡机制，并通过PAC-Bayes分析提供理论支持。

<h3 id="732">[732] Turning Stale Gradients into Stable Gradients: Coherent Coordinate Descent with Implicit Landscape Smoothing for Lightweight Zeroth-Order Optimization</h3>

- **Tags**: Optimization, On-Device Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出CoCD方法，一种确定性、样本高效的零阶优化器，通过隐式景观平滑将历史梯度转化为计算资产。该方法实现每步O(1)查询复杂度，在MLP、CNN和ResNet架构上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="733">[733] One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文调查了15种针对恶意微调的防御方法，发现它们共享一个弱点：掩盖有害行为路径而非移除行为本身。作者开发了统一的自适应攻击方法，成功突破了所有防御机制。

<h3 id="734">[734] SurgicalMamba: Dual-Path SSD with State Regramming for Online Surgical Phase Recognition</h3>

<h3 id="735">[735] Learning Selective Merge Policies for Deadline-Constrained Coded Caching via Deep Reinforcement Learning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; cs.NI

<h3 id="736">[736] Reducing Credit Assignment Variance via Counterfactual Reasoning Paths</h3>

<h3 id="737">[737] How Few-Shot Examples Add Up: A Causal Decomposition of Function Vectors in In-Context Learning</h3>

- **Summary**: 该研究通过因果分解揭示了上下文学习中少样本示例如何形成功能向量，发现n-shot功能向量可由示例级子功能向量的线性组合近似，且模型会根据上下文自适应地重新加权示例贡献。

<h3 id="738">[738] Position: State-of-the-Art Claims Require State-of-the-Art Evidence</h3>

<h3 id="739">[739] L-Drive: Beyond a Single Mapping-Latent Context Drives Time Series Forecasting</h3>

<h3 id="740">[740] BacktestBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Automated Quantitative Strategy Backtesting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17937) (replaced)

<h3 id="741">[741] LivePI: More Realistic Benchmarking of Agents Against Indirect Prompt Injection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17986) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

<h3 id="742">[742] A Simplex Witness Certificate for Constant Collapse in Variational Autoencoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18224) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文研究了变分自编码器(VAE)中的常数坍塌问题，即编码器均值变得与输入无关。作者提出了一种基于单纯形见证者的证书方法来检测坍塌，并提供了逃离坍塌流形的局部方向，在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上验证了方法的有效性。

<h3 id="743">[743] KairosHope: A Next-Generation Time-Series Foundation Model for Specialized Classification via Dual-Memory Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18657) (replaced)

<h3 id="744">[744] ESI-Bench: Towards Embodied Spatial Intelligence that Closes the Perception-Action Loop</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18746) (replaced)

<h3 id="745">[745] Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer</h3>

<h3 id="746">[746] Lying Is Just a Phase: The Hidden Alignment Transition in Language Model Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18838) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Adil Amin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL

<h3 id="747">[747] The Growing Pains of Frontier Models: When Leaderboards Stop Separating and What to Measure Next</h3>

<h3 id="748">[748] CounterFlow: A Two-Phase Inference-Time Sampling for Counterfactual Video Foley Generation</h3>

<h3 id="750">[750] Unlocking the Potential of Continual Model Merging: An ODE Perspective</h3>

<h3 id="751">[751] FineBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Human Activity Understanding</h3>

<h3 id="754">[754] The Devil is in the Condition Numbers: Why is GLU Better than non-GLU Structure?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.20749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20749) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Lyu, Qianqian Xu, Zhiyong Yang, Peisong Wen, Qingming Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Architecture, Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Zemdalk/GLU-NTK)
- **Summary**: 该论文通过神经切线核（NTK）分析揭示了GLU结构优于非门控结构的原因：GLU重塑了NTK谱，导致更小的条件数和更紧凑的特征值分布，从而加速模型收敛。

<h3 id="755">[755] Ordering Matters: Rank-Aware Selective Fusion for Blended Emotion Recognition</h3>

<h3 id="756">[756] Look-Closer-Then-Diagnose: Confidence-Aware Ultrasound VQA via Active Zooming</h3>

<h3 id="763">[763] The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning</h3>

<h3 id="767">[767] CoSPlay: Cooperative Self-Play at Test-Time with Self-Generated Code and Unit Test</h3>
